Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The general variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 123 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 594 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the general space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 123 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 594. QED.
